THIRD-PARTY SOFTWARE NOTICES

@withautonomi/ant-browser-sdk is licensed under MIT OR Apache-2.0; see
LICENSE-MIT and LICENSE-APACHE. This file covers the third-party software in
its bundled WebAssembly module, built from ant-core, and the tools used to
produce it. Each crate's Source link is its exact source, which is how source
is made available where a licence requires it.

The crates below are compiled into this build or used to build it, unmodified
from the source each entry names. Every licence, copyright, notice and
authors file they ship is reproduced unaltered in the TEXTS section,
identical texts once. Where a crate offers a choice of licences, it is
redistributed under the terms named. Each Source link is the exact,
unmodified source of that crate as used for this build, which is also how
source is made available where a licence requires it (for example MPL-2.0).

Targets: wasm32-unknown-unknown
Features: browser-wasm (default features off)

================================================================================
NOTES
================================================================================
blake3 1.8.5: its C, assembly and WebAssembly SIMD implementations are part of BLAKE3 and share the crate's licence.
cc 1.2.63: a build tool; its one C file is compiled on the build machine to detect the C compiler, and nothing from it is linked.
rayon-core 1.13.0: declares a `links` key only to prevent two copies in one build; it builds no native code.
saorsa-pqc 0.5.2: declares MIT OR Apache-2.0 but its published crate and repository contain no licence text; it is redistributed under Apache-2.0, whose text is reproduced from apache.org.
saorsa-transport 0.37.0: the published crate also contains a .minimax/ folder of AI-assistant files (Python tools and .NET DLLs) committed to its repository by mistake; nothing in it is compiled into the WebAssembly module.
syn-solidity 1.6.0: a proc-macro dependency; src/ident/kw.c is a developer script that generated its keyword tables, and is not compiled.
wasm-bindgen-shared 0.2.122: declares a `links` key only to prevent two versions in one build; its build script records version information and builds no native code.

================================================================================

================================================================================
